## 2026-11-24 ~16:00Z — Cost-per-relation angle: a grounded negative + continuous-learning loop (Claude)

### What was done (commands, files, features)

- Owner asked (2026-11-23) to design the cost-per-relation reduction angle (the
  2026-11-20 meta-finding's recommended next step), then (2026-11-24) to "work
  towards continuous improvement and learning".
- Grounded the three multiplicative factors of collection cost against the repo;
  wrote `research/cost_per_relation_analysis_20261123/{README.md,REDTEAM.md}`,
  independently verified (Sonnet Explore) and adversarially red-teamed (Opus).
- Engaged the continuous-learning loop (`tools/lessons.py`): recorded three new
  lessons and one task outcome.

### Decisions made (and why)

- **Cost-per-relation is closed; both tunable factors.** (1) Yield is an EXACT
  base-geometry invariant — KN-FIND-007 (`factor-base-encyclopedia/README.md:631-637`,
  Lean-checked double count); the 1.582 is a *coverage* headroom, not a yield
  multiplier; orbit bases are yield-neutral (cyclotomic falsifier). The Sidon /
  sumset-coverage / additive-energy family is provably capped. (2) Per-attempt is
  engineering, toy-measured only (the root-finding ~10x is a 32-bit-prime
  probe-throughput ratio, mostly refutations, `[pcc:unverified]`, not
  cost-per-relation); the binary port is an n=13 toy, designed-but-unrun,
  budget-blocked.
- **Red-teamed micro-result (conjecture, sharpens KN-FIND-007).** The one escape
  KN-FIND-007 leaves open — biased residual sampling toward the sumset `mD` — is
  closed on the *independent-relations* metric: a known-coefficient move
  `[Da]G+[Db]Q` re-hits `mD` only at the uniform rate, and the only cheap maps
  that are both log-known and `mD`-preserving are Frobenius (lambda) and negation
  (-1), which give the constant `|Aut|`-fold of linearly *dependent* rows. The
  first-draft "lemma" was a tautology + a category error (MITM is check-cost, not
  a redistribution exploit) + literally false on raw membership; corrected per the
  red-team (`REDTEAM.md`). Conditional on ECDLP-hardness, super-constant bias only.
- **Redirect:** the repo's own open door is NOT cost-per-relation but the
  SOLVING DEGREE of the algebraic presentation — a tower / auxiliary-variable
  presentation escapes the Bezout bound (`factor-base-encyclopedia/README.md:1587-1589`).
- Everything `[pcc:unverified]`, desk-only, nothing run, no benchmark, no row.

### Open threads (unfinished, pick up next time)

- **Solving-degree / tower-presentation lever** (PKM towers): the one direction
  with un-closed structural room per the repo's own findings; not yet designed.
- **Corrected §1a** is a conjecture, not a theorem; worth a Lean-style write-up if
  pursued (metric = independent relations; conditional on ECDLP hardness).
- **Binary per-attempt port** (`rootfind_negfold_binary_batch_20261115`) remains
  designed-but-unrun, blocked on the lab monthly budget + L2/replay gates.

### Errors (what went wrong, how it was handled)

- First-draft memo overclaimed: conflated coverage with yield (1.582), misattributed
  the 84-89% collection share (it is oracle-setup share of one pipeline benchmark;
  collection is 84-99% across studies), and called a prime-toy probe-throughput
  ratio a cost-per-relation win. All corrected after the Explore-verify and
  red-team agents; first-draft errors kept visible in the note.
- Applied lessons L-dbdd5a9d8530 (restart merged-PR branch from origin/main),
  L-1e09e78e395f (append to the existing same-day log after fetching main), and
  L-77b44d3fe205 (pcc lint-prose the PR body). New lessons recorded:
  L-4c9e8e66ea68 (check KN-FIND-007 before yield/coverage proposals),
  L-fd9047c1060c (separate the four cost-per-relation quantities),
  L-f2ff07653111 (run the research/ fallback sweep when research-kb is down).

## 2026-11-24 14:19 PST — Nonsquare-action bias gate

### What was done

Proved a full-group correlation identity for any bounded point feature `F`: because `χ_r(3)=-1` on P-192, `2ρ_F=(r-1)^(-1)Σ_d χ_r(d)(F([d]G)-F([3d]G))`. Thus `[3]`-invariant features have exactly zero sign bias, and a feature differing from its `[3]` image on at most `E_3` points has `|ρ_F|≤E_3/(r-1)`. The theorem does not state a fixed-coordinate-offset version. Lange–Shparlinski's result concerns additive field characters and does not directly supply the needed multiplicative one. ## 2026-11-24 21:26 PST — Conditional character-sum grid obstruction

### What was done

Read Cochrane–Yip–Yoo's primary additive-decomposition
paper (`https://arxiv.org/html/2608.02568`), including its
Paley graph conjecture formulation.
For a disjoint paired grid, set `A=I∪(-I)` and `B=J∪(-J)`;
then `A+B⊂Γ` and every sum is nonzero. A nontrivial
multiplicative character trivial on the proper subgroup `Γ`
therefore has bilinear sum exactly `|A||B|`. Any strict
cancellation estimate for those sets would exclude the grid.
The proposed side is about `r^0.22652`; the paper identifies
general cancellation below `r^(1/2)` as open. Recorded this
conditional implication in the report. [pcc:unverified]

### Decisions made

Do not promote the conjectural cancellation to a finite
P-192 grid bound. Keep the additive layout conditional and
seek a specific arithmetic certificate or a different
oriented evaluation route. [pcc:unverified]

character, or a compact sign feature that avoids the grid.
[pcc:unverified]

## 2026-11-24 21:50 PST — y-coset and doubled EDS identity

### What was done

Derived an exact character identity for every even-size scalar
coset `C`: the product of `y([j]P)` over `C` has the same
quadratic character as the product of odd-index division
polynomials over the doubled coset `2C`. The division-polynomial
doubling formula and periodic EDS normalizer supply the proof;
the point-dependent normalizer sign cancels because `|C|` is
even. The fixed order-241 replay now checks the identity at
three markings on each of two curves. [pcc:unverified]

### Decisions made (and why)

- **Cost-per-relation is closed; both tunable factors.** (1) Yield is an EXACT
  base-geometry invariant — KN-FIND-007 (`factor-base-encyclopedia/README.md:631-637`,
  Lean-checked double count); the 1.582 is a *coverage* headroom, not a yield
  multiplier; orbit bases are yield-neutral (cyclotomic falsifier). The Sidon /
  sumset-coverage / additive-energy family is provably capped. (2) Per-attempt is
  engineering, toy-measured only (the root-finding ~10x is a 32-bit-prime
  probe-throughput ratio, mostly refutations, `[pcc:unverified]`, not
  cost-per-relation); the binary port is an n=13 toy, designed-but-unrun,
  budget-blocked.
- **Red-teamed micro-result (conjecture, sharpens KN-FIND-007).** The one escape
  KN-FIND-007 leaves open — biased residual sampling toward the sumset `mD` — is
  closed on the *independent-relations* metric: a known-coefficient move
  `[Da]G+[Db]Q` re-hits `mD` only at the uniform rate, and the only cheap maps
  that are both log-known and `mD`-preserving are Frobenius (lambda) and negation
  (-1), which give the constant `|Aut|`-fold of linearly *dependent* rows. The
  first-draft "lemma" was a tautology + a category error (MITM is check-cost, not
  a redistribution exploit) + literally false on raw membership; corrected per the
  red-team (`REDTEAM.md`). Conditional on ECDLP-hardness, super-constant bias only.
- **Redirect:** the repo's own open door is NOT cost-per-relation but the
  SOLVING DEGREE of the algebraic presentation — a tower / auxiliary-variable
  presentation escapes the Bezout bound (`factor-base-encyclopedia/README.md:1587-1589`).
- Everything `[pcc:unverified]`, desk-only, nothing run, no benchmark, no row.

### Open threads (unfinished, pick up next time)

- **Solving-degree / tower-presentation lever** (PKM towers): the one direction
  with un-closed structural room per the repo's own findings; not yet designed.
- **Corrected §1a** is a conjecture, not a theorem; worth a Lean-style write-up if
  pursued (metric = independent relations; conditional on ECDLP hardness).
- **Binary per-attempt port** (`rootfind_negfold_binary_batch_20261115`) remains
  designed-but-unrun, blocked on the lab monthly budget + L2/replay gates.

### Errors (what went wrong, how it was handled)

- First-draft memo overclaimed: conflated coverage with yield (1.582), misattributed
  the 84-89% collection share (it is oracle-setup share of one pipeline benchmark;
  collection is 84-99% across studies), and called a prime-toy probe-throughput
  ratio a cost-per-relation win. All corrected after the Explore-verify and
  red-team agents; first-draft errors kept visible in the note.
- Applied lessons L-dbdd5a9d8530 (restart merged-PR branch from origin/main),
  L-1e09e78e395f (append to the existing same-day log after fetching main), and
  L-77b44d3fe205 (pcc lint-prose the PR body). New lessons recorded:
  L-4c9e8e66ea68 (check KN-FIND-007 before yield/coverage proposals),
  L-fd9047c1060c (separate the four cost-per-relation quantities),
  L-f2ff07653111 (run the research/ fallback sweep when research-kb is down).

## 2026-11-24 ~16:00Z — Cost-per-relation angle: a grounded negative + continuous-learning loop (Claude)

### What was done (commands, files, features)

- Owner asked (2026-11-23) to design the cost-per-relation reduction angle (the
  2026-11-20 meta-finding's recommended next step), then (2026-11-24) to "work
  towards continuous improvement and learning".
- Grounded the three multiplicative factors of collection cost against the repo;
  wrote `research/cost_per_relation_analysis_20261123/{README.md,REDTEAM.md}`,
  independently verified (Sonnet Explore) and adversarially red-teamed (Opus).
- Engaged the continuous-learning loop (`tools/lessons.py`): recorded three new
  lessons and one task outcome.

### Decisions made (and why)

- **Cost-per-relation is closed; both tunable factors.** (1) Yield is an EXACT
  base-geometry invariant — KN-FIND-007 (`factor-base-encyclopedia/README.md:631-637`,
  Lean-checked double count); the 1.582 is a *coverage* headroom, not a yield
  multiplier; orbit bases are yield-neutral (cyclotomic falsifier). The Sidon /
  sumset-coverage / additive-energy family is provably capped. (2) Per-attempt is
  engineering, toy-measured only (the root-finding ~10x is a 32-bit-prime
  probe-throughput ratio, mostly refutations, `[pcc:unverified]`, not
  cost-per-relation); the binary port is an n=13 toy, designed-but-unrun,
  budget-blocked.
- **Red-teamed micro-result (conjecture, sharpens KN-FIND-007).** The one escape
  KN-FIND-007 leaves open — biased residual sampling toward the sumset `mD` — is
  closed on the *independent-relations* metric: a known-coefficient move
  `[Da]G+[Db]Q` re-hits `mD` only at the uniform rate, and the only cheap maps
  that are both log-known and `mD`-preserving are Frobenius (lambda) and negation
  (-1), which give the constant `|Aut|`-fold of linearly *dependent* rows. The
  first-draft "lemma" was a tautology + a category error (MITM is check-cost, not
  a redistribution exploit) + literally false on raw membership; corrected per the
  red-team (`REDTEAM.md`). Conditional on ECDLP-hardness, super-constant bias only.
- **Redirect:** the repo's own open door is NOT cost-per-relation but the
  SOLVING DEGREE of the algebraic presentation — a tower / auxiliary-variable
  presentation escapes the Bezout bound (`factor-base-encyclopedia/README.md:1587-1589`).
- Everything `[pcc:unverified]`, desk-only, nothing run, no benchmark, no row.

### Open threads (unfinished, pick up next time)

- **Solving-degree / tower-presentation lever** (PKM towers): the one direction
  with un-closed structural room per the repo's own findings; not yet designed.
- **Corrected §1a** is a conjecture, not a theorem; worth a Lean-style write-up if
  pursued (metric = independent relations; conditional on ECDLP hardness).
- **Binary per-attempt port** (`rootfind_negfold_binary_batch_20261115`) remains
  designed-but-unrun, blocked on the lab monthly budget + L2/replay gates.

### Errors (what went wrong, how it was handled)

- First-draft memo overclaimed: conflated coverage with yield (1.582), misattributed
  the 84-89% collection share (it is oracle-setup share of one pipeline benchmark;
  collection is 84-99% across studies), and called a prime-toy probe-throughput
  ratio a cost-per-relation win. All corrected after the Explore-verify and

- Applied lessons L-dbdd5a9d8530 (restart merged-PR branch from origin/main),

### Decisions made (and why)

- The primary future test is one public target at a time with equal four-core allocations for APS and the strongest reproducible rho, cold wall plus summed CPU, memory, generated and cancelled work, retained failures and independent replay. Existing 32/64 rho lanes are batched walks, not physical cores.
- The serial 48-target receipts are development evidence and a cost bound, not parallel performance. The small-field queued solver and its frontier planner do not directly partition the separate 31-bit native S3 loop. No frozen performance panel or EKS run was started.

### Open threads (unfinished, pick up next time)

- Review PR #5176 checks, mark the protocol PR ready and enable auto-merge when the required handoff and gates permit. The broader same-target equal-core implementation and measured crossover goal remains open under a separately frozen, admitted protocol.

### Errors (what went wrong, how it was handled)

- Conductor T-12287, T-12312 and T-12340 successively held the shared tracker or log. The files were edited only after the relevant path cleared or was claimed; this entry waited for the log scope. The initial sparse `git add` refused the new analysis path, resolved with `git add --sparse`. The optional `pcc` executable was absent; the repository's `tools/pcc/pcc.py` lint passed.

## 15:45Z — math-st A5: KLPT with CVP strong approximation (Claude Code, PR #5143)

### What was done
- Preregistered A5 (`PROTOCOLS.md`, 7129e1d) before any code. Copied A4's crate
  to `research/math_st_open_problems_20261119/a5_klpt_cvp/` and changed only
  step (d) (closest points of the determinant-N³ lattice translate) and the e₁
  rule (ℓ^{e₁} ≥ pN³). Added `src/bin/pair.rs` for the paired table.
- Results: control 1 108/108; 1050/1050 runs pass every exact control;
  N identical to A4 in all 1050 pairs. Paired median drops are 0.36–0.46. The
  28-bit medians are 3.280 and 3.313, and the ratio OLS slopes are 2.19 and 2.16.
- Independent recheck: nrd(β) from the recorded coordinates equals ℓ'·ℓ^e,
  1050/1050.

### Decisions made
- H5 recorded as met at 28 bits and on the slope, and under range on one band
  (12-bit ℓ=2, 0.36). It was not adjusted. The shortfall is decomposed from
  recorded stats: log_p(N/2) from the rule change, plus extra e₁ bumps
  (340/237 against 2/1) when the ball holds about π points.

### Open threads
- An e₁ rule with headroom, κpN³, would trade some of the bumps back. Not
  tested.
- P1.6/Q018 not started.

### Errors
- None.
